```
def _conv_self_p(p):

```

For L-BFGS:
```
def _x_to_p(x,n):
   z=np.zeros(n); z[1:]=x; zm=np.nanmax(z); e=np.exp(z-zm); p=e/e.sum(); return p

def _p_to_x(p,n):
   p=np.asarray(p); p=np.clip(p,0,0); s=p.sum(); p=p/s
   lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
   x=lp[1:]-lp[0]
   return x
```
`_compute_fg(x,ctx)`:

```

if cmax <= 0: weights uniform? return.
ex = (q-cmax)/T

f=2*n*soft
h = _h_from_p_w(p,w,n)
g=4*n*h
# Due to FFT, h may have tiny neg, gradient okay. Could np.maximum(h,0) to smooth? We can keep.

# If nonfinite, fail

```

`_multiplicative_polish(p, budget, seed)`:
- n, target p sum1.
- rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
- p_best = p normalized; best_score = _score_p(p_best)
- T_factors = [10.0, 5.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.2]
- Allocate time proportional maybe: each stage 1/6? But final sharp maybe more. Use fractions [0.1,0.15,0.2,0.2,0.2,0.15]. Or simple: loop stages, each stage time = budget/len, last consumes remaining.
- lr = 0.5
- For each stage_factor:
   stage_deadline = min(deadline, t+stage_time)
   it=0; stag=0
   while time < stage_deadline:
      q=_convolution_p(p_current); cmax; if finite.
      w,cmax = _weights(q, factor)
      h=_hot_conv_h(p,w,n)
      g=4*n*h
      d=g - np.mean(g)
      scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
      if scale < 1e-14: stag++; if stag>100 break; continue
      u=d/scale
      if it % 50 ==0 and noise>0: u += rng.normal(0,0.01,n)
      # line search
      best_local = p_current; best_local_score = current_score (fast from q)
      tried = [lr, 0.5*lr, 2*lr] maybe ensure positive. For each lrt in tried:
          cand = p_current * np.exp(-lrt*u)
          if cand has nonfinite or sum<=0 skip.
          cand/=cand.sum()
          sc = _score_p(cand) # computes conv
          if sc < best_local_score: best_local...
       # To reduce FFT, maybe use current_score exact and candidate conv; _score_p recomputes q. Good.
       if best_local_score < best_score: update best.
       if best_local_score <= current_score*(1+1e-9): accept current=best_local; lr *= 1.2 if improved else 0.8; stag=0 else lr*=0.7; stag++
       if stag > 200: perturb p_best? Use p = smooth_perturb(p_best); score; reset lr
   continue
- return p_best,best_score

```
def _upsample_p(p_old, n_new):
    if p_old is None...
```

`smooth_perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.5)`:
- t=linspace; z=sum lowfreq with amp strength/m; p*=exp(z); if strength>1 add local block noise; normalize.

- remaining = budget
- while remaining > 30:
   - pert_level = 0 if first else rng uniform(0.2,0.8)
   - p_work = smooth_perturb(best, strength=pert_level) if not first and perturb_level>0 else best, but if pert worsens too much maybe reject? We can use p_work = normalize((1-α)best + α*noise?) Multiplicative smooth. If pert score > best*1.05 maybe still okay as basin jump.
   - run L-BFGS stages on p_work for time `min(remaining*0.7, 120)`? Update best if improved.
   - run multiplicative polish on best for `min(remaining*0.3, 60)`.
   - remaining update; if no improvement and rounds>3 break.
But this can be expensive. Simpler: one L-BFGS sequence plus polish. Multi-start handles diversity; polishing best enough.

Maybe better: For each start, run L-BFGS then exact top-polish. Keep best. After top k starts at level, do a final "beam" refinement of the level best with larger budget. `run_level` can do:
- Generate starts, top K.
- For each top start, refine with budget_i = base (e.g. 50% of level/k)
- Then if time left, refute global best at level with remaining (additional L-BFGS/perturb/polish).
This is similar previous but with L-BFGS.

Functions:
- `_as_p(p, n=None)`: normalize to sum1; if n given and p length maybe? It just clips finite, if size empty uniform.
```
def _as_p(p, n=None):
   p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).copy()
   if p.size==0:
      if n is None: p=np.ones(1); else p=np.ones(n)
      return p/p.sum()
   p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
   p = np.maximum(p,0.0)
   s=p.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300: p = np.ones_like(p)
   else: p=p/s
   return p
```
- `_score_p(p)`:
```
def _score_p(p):
   p=_as_p(p)
   n=p.size
   c=_convolve_self(p)
   if c.size==0: return inf
   return float(2.0*n*np.max(c))
```
- `_convolve_self(p)`:
```
def _convolve_self(p):
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L); fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N); c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,N)[:L]; c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0); np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c); return c
```
- `_score_from_c(c,n)`.

```
def _gen_binomial(n):
    c=np.empty(n); c[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
       c[i]=c[i-1]*((2*i-1)/(2*i))
    return _as_p(c)
```

```
   r=n-t # if eps? n-1-i+eps = n - t + eps? For i=0,n-i? Use left=t, right=np.arange(n-1,-1,-1,dtype=float)+eps
   a=np.exp(alpha*np.log(left) + beta*np.log(right))
```

Smooth random:
```
def _gen_smooth(n, rng, max_freq=12, amp_scale=1.5, base=None):
   if base is None: base=np.ones(n)/n
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,max_freq+1):
      a=amp_scale*rng.uniform(-1,1)/(0.5+m)
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      z += a*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t+ph)
   # ensure positive: a=base*np.exp(z - np.max(z))
   return _as_p(base*np.exp(z))
```
For base binomial, amp maybe 1.0.

```
def _x_to_p(x,n):
   z=np.empty(n); z[0]=0.0; z[1:]=x
   if not all finite: return None
   zm=np.max(z); e=np.exp(z-zm); s=e.sum(); if s<=0 or not finite: return None; return e/s
```

```
def _p_to_x(p,n):

   lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
   return (lp[1:] - lp[0]).copy()
```

`_hot_h` as above. Need next_pow2 for 3n-2; for n=1? We always n>=128.
```
def _hot_h(p,w,n):
   L=2*n-1
   need=n+L-1
   N=_next_pow2(need)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   w_rev=w[::-1]
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,N)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,N)
   seg=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
   h=seg[::-1]
   h=np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
   # h should be nonnegative; clip small negative
   np.maximum(h,0.0,out=h)
   return h
```
Check stop `2*n-1` equals L, segment length n. For n=1, segment conv[0:1] ok.

`_weights_from_q(q, T_factor)`:
```
def _weights_from_q(q,T_factor):
   cmax=float(np.max(q))
   if cmax <= 0 or not finite:
      w=np.full(q.size,1.0/q.size); return w,cmax
   T=max(cmax/float(T_factor), 1e-12)
   ex=(q-cmax)/T
   ex=np.clip(ex,-745.0,0.0)
   e=np.exp(ex); s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: w= one at max
   else: w=e/s
   soft=cmax + T*np.log(s)
   return w,cmax,soft
```
Need separate for FG where T given. In FG T is absolute. Could call with T.

```
ex=(q-cmax)/T; e...; soft=cmax+T*logsumexp
```

```
def _compute_fg(x, ctx):
   n=ctx['n']; T=ctx['T']
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return (inf, np.zeros(n-1))
   p=_x_to_p(x,n)
   if p is None: return inf,zeros
   q=_convolve_self(p)
   cmax=float(np.max(q))
   if cmax <= 0: return (2*n*0, zeros?) but p sum1 so cmax>0.
   T=max(float(T), cmax/1e6) # avoid zero
   ex=(q-cmax)/T; ex=np.clip(ex,-745,0); e=np.exp(ex); s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: ...
   w=e/s
   soft=cmax + T*math.log(s)
   f=2*n*soft
   h=_hot_h(p,w,n); g=4*n*h; m=float(np.dot(g,p)); grad=p*(g-m); grad[0]=0; gp=grad[1:]
   if not finite gp: gp=zeros
   return (float(f), gp)
```
Note: f uses soft; if T large, f offset; not comparable. fine.

```
def _cached_fg(x, ctx):

       # use maybe if np.allclose? exact.
```

`_polish_top(p, budget, n, seed)`:

```
def _polish_top(p, budget, n, seed):
   p=_as_p(p); best=p; best_score=_score_p(best)
   rng=...
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=...
   factors=[3.0,1.5,0.8,0.4]
   stage_deadline = t0+budget/len
   lr=0.5; accept_count=0
   for factor in factors:
      stage_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + budget*idx/len + budget/len)
      it=0; stale=0
      current=p; current_score=best_score? Wait if start from p maybe not best? Use p input as current; best score separately.
      while time<stage_deadline and stale<300:
         q=_convolve_self(current)
         cmax=float(np.max(q)); if not finite break
         w=... with T=cmax/factor
         h=_hot_h(current,w,n)
         g=4*n*h
         d=g-np.mean(g)
         scale=max(abs(d)); if scale<1e-12: stale++; break?
         u=d/scale
         if rng.random()<0.2: u += rng.normal(0,0.02,n)
         # line search: try [lr, 2*lr, 0.5*lr]
         best_cand=current; best_sc=current_score (from q)
         for lrt in (lr, 2.0*lr, 0.5*lr):
             cand=current*np.exp(-lrt*u)
             cand=_as_p(cand)
             sc=_score_p(cand)
             if sc < best_sc: ...
         if best_sc < best_score: update best
         if best_sc <= current_score*(1+1e-8):
             current=best_cand; current_score=best_sc; lr=min(3.0, lr*1.2); stale=0; accept_count++
         else:
             lr=max(0.05, lr*0.6); stale++
         it++
      # after stage, maybe set current = best if current_score worse? Continue from best to not stuck.
      if best_score < current_score: current=best.copy()
   return best,best_score
```
Cost: each iter 1 q for gradient + up to3 candidate scores =4 conv. For n=512, 4*64ms? maybe 0.25s/100? Fast. For n=4096, 40ms? 40s for100. Okay. Budget maybe 100s =>2500 iters? Might be high. We can reduce candidates: evaluate only lr and 0.5lr; if lr improves try 2lr? Simpler 2 candidates. Let's do 2: [lr, 0.6*lr]. If best is lr and we want larger, lr grows gradually. Good. 3 conv total (current q +2) per iter. Could use current q from previous? We need q for weights; candidate q separate. 3 conv.

`_refine_candidate(p, budget, n, seed)`:

```
def _refine_candidate(p, budget, n, seed):
   p=_as_p(p)
   best_p=p; best_score=_score_p(p)
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
   # L-BFGS if scipy and budget>60? If budget<60 use polish.
   if _HAVE_SCIPY and budget>25:

   # polish exact
   remaining=max(0, deadline-time)
```

This reuses code. Let's implement:
```
def _refine_candidate(p, budget, n, seed):
   best=...; rng
   deadline=...
   rounds_max=2 if budget>300 else 1
   for r in range(rounds_max):
      if time>deadline-10 break
      if r==0: p_work=best.copy(); strength=0
      else: p_work=_smooth_perturb_p(best, rng, 0.3 + 0.2*r); if _score_p(p_work)>best_score*1.1: p_work=best.copy()
      remain=deadline-time
      lbfgs_b = min(remain*0.6, 600)
      polish_b = remain-lbfgs_b
      if _HAVE_SCIPY and lbfgs_b>20: p_work=_lbfgs_stages(p_work, lbfgs_b, n, seed+100*r)
      # if p_work score worse than best, still polish maybe; if terrible, start from best
      if _score_p(p_work) > best_score*1.2: p_work=best.copy()
      if time<deadline-5: p_work=_polish_top(p_work, min(300, deadline-time), n, seed+200*r)
      sc=_score_p(p_work); if sc<best_score: best=p_work; best_score=sc
      if best_score < target? continue for other rounds
```

```
def _run_level(n, budget, parent_p, global_p, seed):
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
   if budget<10: return (parent_p or uniform, score)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   starts=_generate_starts(n,rng,parent_p,global_p)
   if not starts: starts=[(2,uniform)]
   best_p=starts[0][1]; best_score=starts[0][0]
   # choose k
   if n<=512: k=max(1,min(8,int(budget/25)+1))
   elif n<=1024: k=max(1,min(6,int(budget/40)+1))
   elif n<=2048: k=max(1,min(4,int(budget/80)+1))
   else: k=max(1,min(3,int(budget/120)+1))
   # But doing k each with large budget may be too much. time_each = min((deadline - time)/(k-i), 120? maybe cap per start to allow focused.
   max_per_start = 40 if n<=512 else 80 if n<=1024 else 180 if n<=2048 else 400
   for i in range(min(k,len(starts))):
       if time>deadline-20 break
       remain=deadline-time
       time_each = min(max_per_start, remain/(min(k,len(starts))-i))
       if time_each<5: break
       p=starts[i][1]
       # skip if start score much worse than current best? Maybe if score > best*1.02 and i>1? But a good start can improve; still maybe skip top? Use if i>0 and sc > best_score*1.05: continue? Could waste. Let's not skip, but time.

   # focused if remain > 20
   remain=deadline-time
   if remain>30:
      p2,s2=_refine_candidate(best_p, remain, n, seed+9999)
      update
   return best_p,best_score
```

`_gen_smooth` uses base normalized p (not a). If base p sum1, multiply exp; normalize.
```
def _gen_smooth(n, rng, base=None, max_freq=10, amp_scale=1.0):
   if base is None: base=np.ones(n)/n
   base=_as_p(base)
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,max_freq+1):
      amp=amp_scale * rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0) / (0.5 + 0.5*m)
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      z += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t+ph)
   # occasionally add low amplitude high local? no
   p=base*np.exp(z - np.max(z))
   return _as_p(p)
```

Let's code `_polish_top` more concretely:
```
def _polish_top(p, budget, n, seed):
    p = _as_p(p)
    best = p.copy(); best_score = _score_p_assume(p)
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    factors = [4.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.2]
    cur = p.copy(); cur_score=best_score
    lr=0.8
    for idx, fac in enumerate(factors):
       stage_end = t0 + budget*(idx+1)/len(factors)
       if stage_end > deadline: stage_end=deadline
       it=0; stale=0
       while time.monotonic() < stage_end:
          q = _convolve_self(cur)
          cmax = float(np.max(q))
          cur_score = float(2.0*n*cmax)
          if not np.isfinite(cur_score): break
          if cmax <=0: break
          T = max(cmax/fac, 1e-300)
          ex=(q-cmax)/T; ... w
          h=_hot_h(cur,w,n)
          g=4.0*n*h
          d=g - float(np.mean(g))
          scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
          if scale <= 1e-14:
              stale += 1
              if stale > 100: break
              continue
          u=d/scale
          # noise occasionally
          if it % 100 == 0:
              u = u + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
          # line search with two candidates
          base_score = cur_score
          best_cand = cur; best_sc = base_score
          for lrt in (lr, 0.5*lr):
             cand = cur * np.exp(-lrt*u)
             s = cand.sum()
             if s <=0 or not finite: continue
             cand = cand/s
             sc = _score_p_assume(cand)
             if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
             if sc < best_sc:
                 best_cand=cand; best_sc=sc
          if best_sc < best_score:
             best=best_cand.copy(); best_score=best_sc
          if best_sc <= base_score*(1.0 + 1e-10):
             cur=best_cand; cur_score=best_sc
             if best_sc < base_score*(1.0-1e-12):
                 lr = min(3.0, lr*1.15)
             else:
                 lr = max(0.05, lr*0.95)
             stale=0
          else:
             lr = max(0.05, lr*0.65)
             stale += 1
          if stale > 200:
             # perturb best somewhat
             cur = _smooth_perturb_p(best, rng, 0.4)
             cur_score = _score_p_assume(cur)
             lr = 0.8; stale=0
             it=0
          it +=1
       # continue next stage from best if current worse
       if best_score < cur_score:
          cur = best.copy()
          cur_score = best_score
    return best,best_score
```
Potential issue: At stage end, if cur is perturbed best with worse score but good basin, next stage starts from best if worse. Fine.

We want start broad then sharp. So factors should increase? Let's define `T_factor` as `T = cmax / T_factor`: high T_factor => small T sharp. For broad, T_factor small. Detailed code snippets:

```
def _start_key(item):
    return item[0]
```

`_add_start`:
```
def _add_start(starts, p):
    if p is None: return
    p = _as_p(p)
    sc = _score_p_assume(p)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        starts.append((float(sc), p))
```

`_upsample_p`:
```
def _upsample_p(p, n):
    if p is None: return None
    p = np.asarray(p, float)
    m = p.size
    if m == 0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if m == n: return _as_p(p)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return _as_p(out)
```
For large n, np.interp okay.

`_gen_power`:
```
def _gen_power(n, alpha, beta, eps=0.5):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float) + eps
    rt = n - 1 - np.arange(n, dtype=float) + eps
    with np.errstate(divide='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
        a = np.exp(alpha * np.log(t) + beta * np.log(rt))
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a>0), a, 1e-300)
    return _as_p(a)
```
If beta positive, endpoint right high; okay.

`_gen_smooth`:
```
def _gen_smooth(n, rng, base, max_freq=10, amp_scale=1.0):
    base = _as_p(base)
    t = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    z = np.zeros(n, float)
    for m in range(1, max_freq+1):
        amp = amp_scale * rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0) / (0.5 + 0.5*m)
        ph = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
        z += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + ph)
    z = z - np.mean(z) # helps sum?
    a = base * np.exp(z)
    return _as_p(a)
```
If base binomial, exp changes endpoint maybe.

`_gen_block`:
```
def _gen_block(n, rng, blocks=12):
    blocks = max(2, min(blocks, n//2))
    edges = np.unique(np.round(np.linspace(0,n,blocks+1)).astype(int))
    if edges.size < 2: edges=np.array([0,n])
    lengths=np.diff(edges)
    vals=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0,size=len(lengths))
    # occasional zero
    if len(vals)>1 and rng.random()<0.2: vals[int(rng.integers(len(vals)))] = 0.0
    a=np.repeat(vals,lengths)
    return _as_p(a)
```

`_generate_starts`: Be mindful of no lambda. Use local variables.
```
def _generate_starts(n, rng, parent_p, global_p):
    starts=[]
    base_binom = _gen_binomial(n)
    _add_start(starts, np.ones(n))
    _add_start(starts, base_binom)
    _add_start(starts, base_binom[::-1])
    if parent_p is not None: _add_start(starts, _upsample_p(parent_p, n))
    if global_p is not None: _add_start(starts, _upsample_p(global_p, n))
    # power grid
    for alpha in (-0.5, -0.45, -0.4, -0.3, -0.2, -0.1, 0.0):
        for beta in (-0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5):
            _add_start(starts, _gen_power(n, alpha, beta))
    # smooth random
    rnd = 12 if n <= 1024 else 6
    for _ in range(rnd):
       if rng.random() < 0.6:
           _add_start(starts, _gen_smooth(n, rng, base_binom, 10, 1.0))
       else:
           _add_start(starts, _gen_smooth(n, rng, np.ones(n), 12, 1.2))
    for _ in range(max(2, rnd//2)):
       _add_start(starts, _gen_block(n, rng, int(rng.integers(6, 18))))
    # blends of binom and rev
    for mix in (0.75, 0.5, 0.25):
       _add_start(starts, mix*base_binom + (1-mix)*base_binom[::-1])
    if not starts: _add_start(starts, np.ones(n))
    starts.sort(key=_start_key)
    # remove duplicates? not needed
    return starts
```
This may add first power grid 28 starts, each score conv length; for n=4096, 28 conv expensive but okay ~0.5s. Maybe reduce power grid to 12: alpha [-0.5,-0.45,-0.4,-0.3,-0.1,0.0], beta [0.0,0.3]. Keep diversity. Let's set:
```
for alpha in (-0.5,-0.45,-0.4,-0.3,-0.2,0.0):
 for beta in (0.0,0.3,0.6):
```
18. Good.

`_minimize_callback`:
```
def _minimize_callback(xk):
    ctx = _CURRENT_CTX
    if ctx is not None:
        try:
            ctx['last_opt_x'] = np.asarray(xk, dtype=float).copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        raise TimeoutError()
```
Potential: Raising TimeoutError inside scipy callback may be caught? It should propagate. If not, could timeout but no. Good.

`_lbfgs_stage`:
```
def _lbfgs_stage(p, n, T, stage_deadline):
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY: return p
    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE, _CURRENT_CTX
    ctx = {'n': int(n), 'T': float(T), 'scale': max(1000.0, float(n)), 'cache_x': None, 'cache_fg': None, 'last_opt_x': None}
    x0 = _p_to_x(p, int(n))
    if x0 is None: return p
    ctx['last_opt_x'] = x0.copy()
    _CURRENT_CTX = ctx
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = max(time.monotonic(), float(stage_deadline) - 1.0)
    options = {'maxiter': 300, 'maxls': 20, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-7}
    try:
        res = _scipy_minimize(_soft_fun, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=_soft_jac, args=(ctx,), callback=_minimize_callback, options=options)
        xbest = res.x
    except Exception:
        xbest = ctx.get('last_opt_x', x0)
    finally:
        _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0
        _CURRENT_CTX = None
    if xbest is None: return p
    pn = _x_to_p(np.asarray(xbest,float), int(n))
    if pn is not None and np.isfinite(_score_p_assume(pn)):
        return pn
    return p
```
Set maxiter in caller? Could pass maxiter option based on n? Use fixed 300 and timeout. If n=512 stage quick; 300 iter maybe uses whole stage. good.

But `options` fixed 300 even with small budget; callback stops at stage_deadline. Good. For n=4096, 300 iter maybe timeout. okay.

`_lbfgs_stages`:
```
def _lbfgs_stages(p, budget, n, seed):
    p=_as_p(p); n=int(n)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_p_assume(p)
    if budget <= 10: return best,best_score
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
    mults=[5.0,2.5,1.2,0.6,0.3,0.15]
    cur=p.copy(); cur_score=best_score
    for idx, tm in enumerate(mults):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2: break
       stage_deadline = t0 + budget*(idx+1)/len(mults)
       if stage_deadline > deadline: stage_deadline=deadline
       qmax = float(np.max(_convolve_self(cur)))
       if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax<=0: break
       T = max(qmax*tm, 1e-12)
       p_new = _lbfgs_stage(cur, n, T, stage_deadline)
       if p_new is None: break
       p_new = _as_p(p_new)
       sc = _score_p_assume(p_new)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
           best = p_new.copy(); best_score = sc
       if np.isfinite(sc):
           cur = p_new
           cur_score = sc
           # if cur much worse than best and not first stage, reset to best to avoid bad basin
           if sc > best_score * 1.25 and idx > 0:
               cur = best.copy(); cur_score = best_score
    return best,best_score
```
Note: If first stage worsens but maybe needed, keep. If second worsens >1.25, reset. Good.

`_polish_top` as above. Need use budget maybe 0. If no budget, return.

`_refine_candidate`:
```
def _refine_candidate(p, budget, n, seed):
   p=_as_p(p); n=int(n)
   best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_p_assume(p)
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
   if budget < 5 or not np.isfinite(best_score): return best,best_score
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   # Round 0: direct

         if s2 < best_score: best=p2; best_score=s2

              p_work = best.copy()
          # second refine shorter
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget=2400, **kwargs):
   t0=time.monotonic(); total=max(0,float(budget))
   if total < 20: return _sanitize_for_return(None, 1024)
   search_deadline=t0+max(10, total-120)
   # global_p
   global_p = None
   try:
       hg = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if hg is not None:
           global_p = _as_p(np.asarray(hg, float))
   except Exception:
       global_p=None
   levels = [512,1024,2048,4096]
   weights = [0.08,0.15,0.27,0.50]
   # If total small adjust
   search_budget = max(10, search_deadline - t0)
   # Ensure weights sum to 1
   pool=[]
   parent=global_p
   for idx,n in enumerate(levels):
       b = search_budget * weights[idx]
       if b < 15 and idx < len(levels)-1: 
           # skip small level if too little? maybe allocate
           b = 0? 
       if time.monotonic() > search_deadline - 30: break
       if parent is None:
           parent = np.ones(n, dtype=float)/n
       try:
           p,sc = _run_level(n, b, parent, global_p, int(seed+1000*idx))
       except Exception:
           p,sc = None,float('inf')
       if p is None: p=_as_p(parent if parent.size==n else np.ones(n)/n)
       if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=_score_p_assume(p)
       _add_pool(pool,p,sc)
       if sc < best_fast: best...
       parent = p
   # if still time, refine largest best?

       n_best = len(best_p) if best_p is not None else 1024
       p2,s2=_refine_candidate(best_p, min(remain, 600), n_best, seed+99999)
       update pool
   # exact evaluation
   exact_best_list=None; exact_best_score=inf
   candidates=sorted(pool,key=_start_key)[:5]
   exact_deadline=t0+total-10
   for p,sc in candidates:
       if time.monotonic() > exact_deadline - 5: break

          exact_best_score=ex; exact_best_list=arr
       print maybe
   if exact_best_list is None:
       # fallback sanitize best_p

       exact_best_list=arr
   return exact_best_list
```

```
def _add_pool(pool,p,sc):
   if p is None or not np.isfinite(sc): return
   p=np.asarray(p,float).copy()
   pool.append((float(sc),p))
   pool.sort(key=_start_key)
   if len(pool)>12: pool.pop()
```

```
def _run_level(n,budget,parent_p,global_p,seed):
   n=int(n); budget=float(max(0,budget))
   if budget < 10:
       if parent_p is not None: p=_upsample_p(parent_p,n)
       else: p=np.ones(n)/n
       return _as_p(p), _score_p_assume(p)
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   starts=_generate_starts(n,rng,parent_p,global_p)
   if not starts: starts=[(2.0, np.ones(n)/n)]
   best_p=starts[0][1].copy(); best_score=starts[0][0]
   print(...)

   k = min(k, 5 if n<=1024 else 3?) maybe.
   time_each = (budget*0.7)/k if k>1 else budget*0.55
   time_each = min(time_each, 600.0)
   focus_reserve = max(30.0, budget*0.25)
   for i,p in enumerate(starts[:k]):

      p2,s2=_refine_candidate(best_p, remain, n, seed+999)

   return best_p,best_score
```

```
def _refine_candidate(p, budget, n, seed):
    n=int(n); p=_as_p(p)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_p_assume(p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): return best, best_score
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0,float(budget))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for r in range(2):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
        if r == 0:
            p_work=best.copy()
        else:
            p_work=_smooth_perturb_p(best, rng, 0.25 + 0.25*r)
            scw = _score_p_assume(p_work)
            if not np.isfinite(scw) or scw > best_score * 1.15:
                p_work=best.copy()
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if _HAVE_SCIPY and rem > 30:
            lb_b = min(rem*0.55, 700.0)
            p2,s2=_lbfgs_stages(p_work, lb_b, n, seed+100*r)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best=p2.copy(); best_score=s2
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score*1.3:
                p_work=p2.copy()
            else:
                # if p_work still perturbed and bad, next polish from best? 
                pass
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if rem > 8:
            p_polish = p_work if (p_work is not None and _score_p_assume(p_work) < best_score*1.4) else best.copy()
            p2,s2=_polish_top(p_polish, rem, n, seed+200*r)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best=p2.copy(); best_score=s2
    return best,best_score
```

```
  if x.size != n-1: return None
```

Now, `_convolve_self_fp` code:
```
def _convolve_self_fp(p,n,N):
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    q=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,N)[:2*n-1]
    q=np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    np.maximum(q,0.0,out=q)
    return q, fp
```
Need ensure p float contiguous. okay.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

Method:
- Work on the probability simplex p = a / sum(a).  For sum(p)=1 the score is
  2n * max((p*p)_k).
- Generate ambitious starts: previous solution if available, discrete
  inverse-sqrt / central-binomial prototypes, power-law prototypes, and random
  smooth/block perturbations.
- Refine with multi-start multi-resolution optimization:
    * smooth log-sum-exp objective on softmax variables, optimized by L-BFGS-B
      when SciPy is available;
    * multiplicative mirror-polish with annealed peak weighting.
- Keep a candidate pool and validate the best candidates with the exact
  evaluator before returning.
"""

import numpy as np
import math
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

_MAX_VALUE = 1000.0
_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0
_CURRENT_CTX = None


def _start_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _pool_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = max(1, int(m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _as_p(p):
    """Normalize to a non-negative probability vector with tiny positive floor."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).copy()
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(1024, dtype=float)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(p)
        s = float(p.size)
    p = p / s
    floor = 1e-13 / max(1, p.size)
    if p.size and p.min() < floor:
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        p = p / float(p.sum())
    return p


def _upsample_p(p, n):
    """Linearly interpolate a probability shape to length n, then renormalize."""
    n = int(n)
    if p is None or n <= 0:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if p.size == 0:
        return _as_p(np.ones(n))
    if p.size == n:
        return _as_p(p)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(new, old, p)
    return _as_p(out)


def _convolve_self_fp(p, n, N):
    """Linear self-convolution plus FFT of p, padded to length N."""
    n = int(n)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(p, dtype=float), N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    return q, fp


def _convolve_self(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    q, _ = _convolve_self_fp(p, n, N)
    return q


def _score_p_assume(p):
    """Score for p already normalized to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return float("inf")
    q = _convolve_self(p)
    if q.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(q)))


def _score_p(p):
    p = _as_p(p)
    return _score_p_assume(p)


def _add_start(starts, p):
    if p is None:
        return
    p = _as_p(p)
    sc = _score_p_assume(p)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        starts.append((float(sc), p))


def _gen_binomial(n):
    """Discrete inverse-sqrt prototype: coefficients of (1-x)^(-1/2)."""
    n = int(n)
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i] = c[i - 1] * ((2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i))
    return _as_p(c)


def _gen_power(n, alpha, beta, eps=0.5):
    n = int(n)
    left = np.arange(n, dtype=float) + eps
    right = (n - 1 - np.arange(n, dtype=float)) + eps
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        a = np.exp(alpha * np.log(left) + beta * np.log(right))
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 1e-300)
    return _as_p(a)


def _gen_smooth(n, rng, base, max_freq=10, amp_scale=1.0):
    n = int(n)
    base = _as_p(base)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for m in range(1, int(max_freq) + 1):
        amp = amp_scale * float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) / (0.5 + 0.5 * float(m))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t + ph)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    a = base * np.exp(z)
    return _as_p(a)


def _gen_block(n, rng, blocks=12):
    n = int(n)
    max_blocks = max(2, n // 2)
    blocks = int(max(2, min(int(blocks), max_blocks)))
    edges = np.unique(np.round(np.linspace(0.0, n, blocks + 1)).astype(int))
    if edges.size < 2:
        edges = np.array([0, n], dtype=int)
    lengths = np.diff(edges)
    vals = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0, size=lengths.size)
    if vals.size > 1 and rng.random() < 0.2:
        vals[int(rng.integers(vals.size))] = 0.0
    a = np.repeat(vals, lengths)
    return _as_p(a)


def _generate_starts(n, rng, parent_p, global_p):
    n = int(n)
    starts = []

    _add_start(starts, np.ones(n))
    base_binom = _gen_binomial(n)
    _add_start(starts, base_binom)
    _add_start(starts, base_binom[::-1])

    if parent_p is not None:
        _add_start(starts, _upsample_p(parent_p, n))
    if global_p is not None:
        _add_start(starts, _upsample_p(global_p, n))

    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    _add_start(starts, t + 1.0)
    _add_start(starts, t[::-1] + 1.0)
    _add_start(starts, t * (n - 1.0 - t) + 1.0)

    for alpha in (-0.5, -0.45, -0.4, -0.3, -0.2, 0.0):
        for beta in (0.0, 0.3, 0.6):
            _add_start(starts, _gen_power(n, alpha, beta))

    for mix in (0.75, 0.5, 0.25):
        _add_start(starts, mix * base_binom + (1.0 - mix) * base_binom[::-1])

    rnd = 12 if n <= 1024 else 7
    for _ in range(rnd):
        if rng.random() < 0.6:
            _add_start(starts, _gen_smooth(n, rng, base_binom, max_freq=10, amp_scale=1.0))
        else:
            _add_start(starts, _gen_smooth(n, rng, np.ones(n), max_freq=12, amp_scale=1.2))

    for _ in range(max(2, rnd // 2)):
        _add_start(starts, _gen_block(n, rng, int(rng.integers(6, 18))))

    if not starts:
        _add_start(starts, np.ones(n))

    starts.sort(key=_start_key)
    return starts


def _smooth_perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.5):
    p = _as_p(p)
    n = p.size
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for m in range(1, 5):
        amp = float(strength) * float(rng.uniform(0.6, 1.4)) / (0.5 + 0.5 * float(m))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t + ph)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    out = p * np.exp(z)
    if n > 16 and rng.random() < 0.45:
        width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 200), max(2, n // 10 + 1)))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        sp = min(n, st + width)
        out[st:sp] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * float(strength), sp - st))
    return _as_p(out)


def _p_to_x(p, n):
    p = _as_p(p)
    if p.size != n:
        p = _upsample_p(p, n)
        p = _as_p(p)
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    return (lp[1:] - lp[0]).astype(float).copy()


def _x_to_p(x, n):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size != max(0, n - 1):
        return None
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    z[0] = 0.0
    if n > 1:
        z[1:] = x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return None
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    p = e / s
    floor = 1e-13 / max(1, n)
    if p.min() < floor:
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        p = p / float(p.sum())
    return p


def _hot_h(p, w, n):
    """
    h_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i}.
    Computed as a cross-correlation by FFT.
    """
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    need = n + L - 1
    N = _next_pow2(need)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(p, dtype=float), N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(w[::-1], dtype=float), N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, N)
    seg = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    h = seg[::-1]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)
    return h


def _compute_fg(x, ctx):
    n = int(ctx["n"])
    T = float(ctx["T"])
    scale_factor = float(ctx.get("scale", max(1000.0, float(n))))
    zero_grad = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=float)

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return float("inf"), zero_grad

    p = _x_to_p(x, n)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf"), zero_grad

    # Use one large FFT for both q=p*p and the gradient cross-correlation.
    L = 2 * n - 1
    need = n + L - 1
    N = _next_pow2(need)
    q, fp = _convolve_self_fp(p, n, N)
    cmax = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0 * scale_factor, zero_grad

    T = max(T, cmax * 1e-6, 1e-300)
    ex = (q - cmax) / T
    ex = np.clip(ex, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(ex)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        int(np.argmax(q))
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        logsum = 0.0
    else:
        w = e / s
        logsum = math.log(s)

    soft = cmax + T * logsum
    f = 2.0 * float(n) * soft

    h = _hot_h(p, w, n)
    g = 4.0 * float(n) * h
    m = float(np.dot(g, p))
    grad_p = p * (g - m)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)):
        grad_p = np.zeros_like(grad_p)
    grad_param = grad_p[1:]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_param)):
        grad_param = zero_grad

    return float(scale_factor * f), (scale_factor * grad_param).astype(float)


def _cached_fg(x, ctx):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    last = ctx.get("cache_x")
    if last is not None and last.shape == x.shape:
        try:
            if np.array_equal(last, x):
                return ctx.get("cache_fg")
        except Exception:
            pass
    fg = _compute_fg(x, ctx)
    ctx["cache_x"] = x.copy()
    ctx["cache_fg"] = fg
    return fg


def _soft_fun(x, ctx):
    fg = _cached_fg(x, ctx)
    return fg[0]


def _soft_jac(x, ctx):
    fg = _cached_fg(x, ctx)
    return fg[1]


def _minimize_callback(xk):
    ctx = _CURRENT_CTX
    if ctx is not None:
        try:
            ctx["last_opt_x"] = np.asarray(xk, dtype=float).copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        raise TimeoutError


def _lbfgs_stage(p, n, T, stage_deadline):
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return p
    n = int(n)
    stage_deadline = float(stage_deadline)
    if stage_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 0.5:
        return p

    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE, _CURRENT_CTX

    ctx = {
        "n": n,
        "T": float(T),
        "scale": max(1000.0, float(n)),
        "cache_x": None,
        "cache_fg": None,
        "last_opt_x": None,
    }
    x0 = _p_to_x(p, n)
    if x0 is None:
        return p
    ctx["last_opt_x"] = x0.copy()

    _CURRENT_CTX = ctx
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = max(time.monotonic(), stage_deadline - 0.75)

    options = {
        "maxiter": 320,
        "maxls": 25,
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-7,
    }

    try:
        res = _scipy_minimize(
            _soft_fun,
            x0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=_soft_jac,
            args=(ctx,),
            callback=_minimize_callback,
            options=options,
        )
        xbest = res.x
    except Exception:
        xbest = ctx.get("last_opt_x", x0)
    finally:
        _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0
        _CURRENT_CTX = None

    if xbest is None:
        return p
    pn = _x_to_p(np.asarray(xbest, dtype=float), n)
    if pn is not None:
        sc = _score_p_assume(pn)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            return pn
    return p


def _lbfgs_stages(p, budget, n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    p = _as_p(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_p_assume(p)
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget))
    if budget <= 5.0 or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return best, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget
    cur = p.copy()
    cur_score = best_score

    t_mults = (5.0, 2.5, 1.2, 0.6, 0.3, 0.12)
    for idx, tm in enumerate(t_mults):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        stage_deadline = t0 + budget * float(idx + 1) / float(len(t_mults))
        stage_deadline = min(stage_deadline, deadline)
        if stage_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 0.75:
            continue

        qmax = float(np.max(_convolve_self(cur)))
        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
            break
        T = max(qmax * float(tm), 1e-12)

        pn = _lbfgs_stage(cur, n, T, stage_deadline)
        if pn is None:
            break
        pn = _as_p(pn)
        sc = _score_p_assume(pn)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_score:
                best = pn.copy()
                best_score = sc
            cur = pn
            cur_score = sc
            if sc > best_score * 1.25 and idx > 0:
                cur = best.copy()
                cur_score = best_score

    return best, best_score


def _polish_top(p, budget, n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    p = _as_p(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_p_assume(p)
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget))
    if budget <= 2.0 or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    cur = p.copy()
    cur_score = best_score
    lr = 0.7

    t_mults = (3.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.05)
    for idx, tm in enumerate(t_mults):
        stage_end = t0 + budget * float(idx + 1) / float(len(t_mults))
        stage_end = min(stage_end, deadline)
        it = 0
        stale = 0

        while time.monotonic() < stage_end:
            q = _convolve_self(cur)
            cmax = float(np.max(q))
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
            if not np.isfinite(cur_score) or cmax <= 0.0:
                break

            T = max(cmax * float(tm), 1e-300)
            ex = (q - cmax) / T
            ex = np.clip(ex, -745.0, 0.0)
            e = np.exp(ex)
            s = float(e.sum())
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                w = np.zeros_like(q)
                w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
            else:
                w = e / s

            h = _hot_h(cur, w, n)
            g = 4.0 * float(n) * h
            d = g - float(np.mean(g))
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-14:
                stale += 1
                if stale > 150:
                    break
                continue

            u = d / scale
            if it % 120 == 0:
                u = u + rng.normal(0.0, 0.012, n)

            base_score = cur_score
            best_cand = cur
            best_sc = base_score
            best_lr = lr

            for lrt in (lr, 0.55 * lr):
                cand = cur * np.exp(-lrt * u)
                cs = float(cand.sum())
                if cs <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cs):
                    continue
                cand = cand / cs
                sc = _score_p_assume(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_cand = cand.copy()
                    best_lr = lrt

            if best_sc < best_score:
                best = best_cand.copy()
                best_score = best_sc

            if best_sc <= base_score * (1.0 + 1e-11):
                cur = best_cand
                cur_score = best_sc
                if best_sc < base_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                    lr = min(3.0, float(best_lr) * 1.12)
                else:
                    lr = max(0.05, float(best_lr))
                stale = 0
            else:
                lr = max(0.05, float(lr) * 0.62)
                stale += 1

            if stale > 220:
                cur = _smooth_perturb_p(best, rng, 0.45)
                cur_score = _score_p_assume(cur)
                lr = 0.75
                stale = 0
                it = 0

            it += 1

        if best_score < cur_score:
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_score = best_score

    return best, best_score


def _refine_candidate(p, budget, n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    p = _as_p(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_p_assume(p)
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget))
    if budget <= 3.0 or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    for r in range(2):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
            break

        if r == 0:
            p_work = best.copy()
        else:
            p_work = _smooth_perturb_p(best, rng, 0.25 + 0.25 * float(r))
            scw = _score_p_assume(p_work)
            if not np.isfinite(scw) or scw > best_score * 1.15:
                p_work = best.copy()

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if _HAVE_SCIPY and rem > 30.0:
            lb_b = min(rem * 0.55, 700.0)
            p2, s2 = _lbfgs_stages(p_work, lb_b, n, seed + 100 * r)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best = p2.copy()
                best_score = s2
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score * 1.30:
                p_work = p2.copy()

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 8.0:
            scw = _score_p_assume(p_work)
            p_polish = p_work if (np.isfinite(scw) and scw < best_score * 1.40) else best.copy()
            p2, s2 = _polish_top(p_polish, rem, n, seed + 200 * r)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best = p2.copy()
                best_score = s2

    return best, best_score


def _run_level(n, budget, parent_p, global_p, seed):
    n = int(n)
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget))
    if budget < 12.0:
        if parent_p is not None:
            p = _upsample_p(parent_p, n)
        else:
            p = np.ones(n) / float(n)
        p = _as_p(p)
        return p, _score_p_assume(p)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    starts = _generate_starts(n, rng, parent_p, global_p)
    if not starts:
        starts = [(2.0, np.ones(n) / float(n))]

    best_p = starts[0][1].copy()
    best_score = starts[0][0]
    print(f"level {n}: start pool best={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    base_per_run = max(25.0, min(320.0, float(n) / 16.0 + 25.0))
    k = int(min(len(starts), max(1, int((budget * 0.70) / base_per_run))))
    if n <= 1024:
        k = min(k, 6)
    else:
        k = min(k, 3)
    k = max(1, k)

    if k > 1:
        time_each = (budget * 0.70) / float(k)
    else:
        time_each = budget * 0.55
    time_each = min(time_each, 650.0)

    focus_reserve = max(30.0, budget * 0.25)

    for i in range(k):
        if i >= len(starts):
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - focus_reserve:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        tt = min(time_each, rem - focus_reserve)
        if tt < 8.0:
            break
        p0, sc0 = starts[i]
        try:
            p1, sc1 = _refine_candidate(p0, tt, n, int(seed * 13 + i * 101))
        except Exception:
            p1, sc1 = p0, float("inf")
        if p1 is None:
            p1 = p0
        if not np.isfinite(sc1):
            sc1 = _score_p_assume(p1)
        if sc1 < best_score:
            best_p = p1.copy()
            best_score = sc1
            print(f"level {n}: improved by start {i} -> {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 15.0:
        try:
            p1, sc1 = _refine_candidate(best_p, rem, n, int(seed + 9973))
        except Exception:
            p1, sc1 = best_p, best_score
        if p1 is None:
            p1 = best_p
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_p = p1.copy()
            best_score = sc1

    print(f"level {n}: after focus best={best_score:.9f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
    return best_p, best_score


def _add_pool(pool, p, score):
    if p is None:
        return
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).copy()
    pool.append((sc, p))
    pool.sort(key=_pool_key)
    if len(pool) > 12:
        pool.pop()


def _sanitize_for_return(p, n=None):
    if p is None:
        n = 1024 if n is None else int(n)
        return [1.0] * max(1, n)

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if n is None:
        n = int(p.size)
    else:
        n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 1024
    if p.size != n:
        p = _upsample_p(p, n)
        p = _as_p(p)

    a = p * float(n)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    amax = float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
    if amax > _MAX_VALUE:
        a *= _MAX_VALUE / amax

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    if a.size and amax > 0.0:
        scale = float(n) / s
        if amax * scale <= _MAX_VALUE:
            a *= scale
        elif amax < _MAX_VALUE:
            a *= _MAX_VALUE / amax

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def _get_global_p():
    try:
        hg = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if hg is None:
            return None
        arr = np.asarray(hg, dtype=float)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        return _as_p(arr)
    except Exception:
        return None


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main entry point. Searches under the time budget and returns a valid list."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    grand_total = max(0.0, float(budget))
    global_p = _get_global_p()

    if grand_total < 60.0:
        if global_p is not None:
            return _sanitize_for_return(global_p, len(global_p))
        return [1.0] * 1024

    exact_reserve = min(120.0, max(15.0, grand_total * 0.05))
    search_deadline = t0 + max(20.0, grand_total - exact_reserve)
    search_budget = max(10.0, search_deadline - t0)

    if search_budget < 250.0:
        levels = [512, 1024]
        weights = [0.35, 0.65]
    elif search_budget < 700.0:
        levels = [512, 1024, 2048]
        weights = [0.20, 0.35, 0.45]
    else:
        levels = [512, 1024, 2048, 4096]
        weights = [0.08, 0.15, 0.25, 0.52]

    pool = []
    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    if global_p is not None:
        sc = _score_p_assume(global_p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_p = global_p.copy()
            best_score = sc
            _add_pool(pool, best_p, best_score)
            print(f"global previous fast score={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    for idx, n in enumerate(levels):
        if time.monotonic() > search_deadline - 5.0:
            break
        remain = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remain <= 20.0:
            break
        total_w = float(sum(weights[idx:]))
        if total_w <= 0.0:
            continue
        b = remain * float(weights[idx]) / total_w
        if idx < len(levels) - 1:
            b = min(b, remain * 0.70)
            b = max(15.0, b)
        else:
            b = remain
        if b <= 5.0:
            continue

        parent = global_p if (best_p is None) else best_p
        try:
            p, sc = _run_level(int(n), float(b), parent, global_p, int(seed + 1000 * idx))
        except Exception:
            p, sc = None, float("inf")

        if p is None:
            if parent is not None:
                p = _upsample_p(parent, int(n))
            else:
                p = np.ones(int(n)) / float(n)
            p = _as_p(p)
            sc = _score_p_assume(p)

        _add_pool(pool, p, sc)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

    if not pool:
        fallback = np.ones(512, dtype=float) / 512.0
        _add_pool(pool, fallback, _score_p_assume(fallback))
        best_p = fallback.copy()
        best_score = float(pool[0][0])

    exact_deadline = t0 + grand_total - 2.0
    exact_best_seq = None
    exact_best_score = float("inf")

    candidates = sorted(pool, key=_pool_key)[:6]
    large_evals = 0

    for sc, p in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > exact_deadline - 3.0:
            break
        n = int(p.size)
        if n > 2048 and large_evals >= 3:
            continue
        try:
            arr = _sanitize_for_return(p, n)
            ex = float(evaluate_sequence(arr))
        except Exception:
            ex = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(ex):
            if ex < exact_best_score:
                exact_best_score = ex
                exact_best_seq = arr
                print(f"exact eval n={n} score={ex:.9f}", flush=True)
            if n > 2048:
                large_evals += 1
            # Keep searching only if we still can beat current fastest or have no exact yet.
            if exact_best_score < best_score * 0.99999 and time.monotonic() > exact_deadline - 20.0:
                break

    if exact_best_seq is None:
        if best_p is None:
            best_p = np.ones(512, dtype=float) / 512.0
        exact_best_seq = _sanitize_for_return(best_p, int(best_p.size))

    return exact_best_seq
```
